          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "An important part of GregTech is its Ore Processing Mechanics, but it can get pretty complicated, so this chapter is here to help!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To start with, you should be aware of the Ore Processing Diagram tab in EMI - Press the Show Uses key (default U) on any item related to ore processing and look for the tab with the vanilla Iron Ore icon. This diagram may look overwhelming at first, but we'll take it step by step.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "A major part of GregTech philosophy is that there's not just one way to solve a problem, but we've marked everything important with a star, so if you're completely lost, try following those!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To Process, Ore Not To Process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I agree to not just dump everything in a furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While looking at EMI, you've probably noticed that your Macerator or Millstone lists more outputs than what you're actually getting.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech doesn't communicate this very well, but these extra slots are only available in HV. Once you make it to that tier, the HV macerator becomes incredibly powerful, giving a huge amount of bonus byproducts from each ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I can get even more from my ores!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hv_macerator)item(greate:stainless_steel_crushing_wheel))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Either an HV Macerator or HS Crushing Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Crushing with byproducts",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathing certain ores in Mercury can give some other useful byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Mercury can be easily obtained from Redstone or Cinnabar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples of crushed ores which are good to process in Mercury.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Don't stick your finger in it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Mercury",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Once you get to LV, you may be interested in using a Chemical Bath with Sodium persulfate to get some extra byproducts.           "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

        },

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line shouldn't be needed before IV, but it will require purified Sphalerite and Galena.",

        },
        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platinum Line will be your first real challenge to process, providing you with essential platinum-group metals: Platinum, Palladium, Ruthenium, Rhodium, Iridium, and Osmium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While there's easier ways to obtain Platinum and Palladium, this line will be mandatory for accessing the others.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platline can be started at HV, but you won’t be able to complete it fully until later in EV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",

          "text": "hv__high_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Energium Crystals store a significant amount of EU. They're also arguably cheaper.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Science-fiction batteries!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Super Batteries",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Autoclave - despite being optional - is a machine you'll definitively want to grab.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This unlocks unique Batteries that are far superior to their standard counterparts.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Home-grown batteries",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Autoclave",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Chemical Bath is used to dye Lenses using Chemical Dye. This will let you engrave more types of Wafers, and cool other kinds of Hot Ingots.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Please don't bathe with your toaster...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Bathing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You now have access to two new Maintenance Hatches.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Automatic Maintenance Hatch is pretty simple - it eliminates the need for Maintenance, forever.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Configurable Maintenance Hatch is more interesting. You can configure it to cut off 10%% duration on recipes, at the cost of making Maintenance happen three times as fast. That is 16 real hours of activity. Additionally, you can use it on Multiblock Generators to increase the duration of the recipe, which indirectly increases their fuel efficiency by 10%%!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Reminder: You can put Tape in the maintenance Hatch to automatically fix problems.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: The Automatic Maintenance Hatch texture and mechanic comes from TecTech, an addon mod originally made for GregTech: New Horizons.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You'll see more from TecTech if you stick with us with the late game.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "I have become maintenance, eater of tape",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Advanced Maintenance",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Just like MV, you'll need to make higher-tier components.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The painful Flawless Gems have been replaced with slightly-more-painful Vitrified Ender Pearls. Also notice that the Motors require 2x Silver Cables.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hulls and Machines use Gold Cables.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, a use for that stockpile of Gold!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Tips to make life less painful:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use Electrotine decomposition and Ore Processing for Electrum.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use the Chemical Bath with Mercury for Gold and Silver from a wide variety of ores.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We call this \"fun\"",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Assembler is the next step in progression, but also unlocks a handful of useful utilities - check around this quest for more.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need this HV Assembler to make your Rocket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Assembler",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You'll want to grab these two lenses. The Simple System on Chip will be used for the best ULV Circuit recipe, while the LPIC Wafer is required for your next Energy Hatch.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "I see the world in colour",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "ULV Circuits are used in some AE2 recipes and Create recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Best ULV circuits!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Low Power Integrated Circuit requires an HV Cutter inside your Cleanroom.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Energy Hatches will be nice additions so you can run your Multiblocks (mainly your Electric Blast Furnace and Large Chemical Reactor) directly off a HV line.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Noticing a pattern here?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Upgrade your EBF II",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Chemical Reactor unlocks a ton of new recipes. Ah, who doesn't love the ever-growing demand on more advanced Chemistry?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: We've actually unlocked a ton of new content by reaching HV. However, not every processing line or item is crucial right now. It'd make the HV chapter far too dense if we included everything!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You could still check out the EV Chapter if you want to push yourself.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Chemistry",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Titanium requires a slightly more involved process than the previous materials to process. To get started, you'll need Rutile, which can be obtained by processing Ilmenite that you can get from Bauxite, Aluminium, Armalcolite and Desh, which can be found in massive quantities on The Moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once you've gotten enough Rutile, your journey to Titanium continues in the next quest. There's no need to rush things.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: Ore processing for Bauxite is ridiculously good! It can easily triples your Rutile yield.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "...means it WASN'T Titanium!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Almost Titanium",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To get your grubby hands on some Titanium, Rutile must first be reduced to Titanium Tetrachloride in an HV Chemical Reactor, then reduced again in an EBF with Magnesium to finally arrive at Titanium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Carbon, Chlorine, Oxygen, and Magnesium used in the process can be perfectly recovered from the byproduct Carbon Monoxide and Magnesium Chloride with no loss. If you set up a system to recover them, the only material that you'll need to provide for Titanium is Rutile!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Titanium Tetrachloride also acts as a catalyst in the production of plastics. A touch of TiCl₄ in the polymerisation process can increase the polymer yield to 133%%. Naturally, this is completely optional.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Is it titanium yet?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Click here to complete this quest/task",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Almost Titanium²",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "By this point, normal Cables have such insignificant loss that superconductors aren't a requirement. That being said, they're still useful for being able to split and combine cables on the fly, and these ones are half oxygen so they're cheap too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Superconductors are still required to craft Field Generators.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Superconductors",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Nichrome Heating Coils increase the temperature of your Blast Furnace to 3,600K.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "These will be required to upgrade your primary EBF so you can make Titanium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: In the original GregTech 5, there were only three types of coils. This was the last tier.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "A long way to go before we beat the Sun",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Nichrome Coils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Titanium has the highest corrosion resistance and strength-to-density ratio of any metallic element, so it's only natural that we'll be using it as the main material in our EV machinery.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",

          "text": "Throughout the moon, There are large, inhabited underground villages. These villages contain an alien form of the earth villager, who call themselves Lunarians. Unlike their earth counterpart, Lunarian don't need oxygen and can survive the chilling environment of the moon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",

          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {

          "text": "Lunarian have different trades than earth villagers, featuring planet-specific materials, space suits and oxygen gear.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",

    {
      "id": "pack:tfg/wild_animals",
      "label": "Wild Animals",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Wild Animals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The world of TFC is full of animal life. Some animals are here to help, and some are incredibly dangerous. This section is about wild animals. For information on livestock, animals that can give you items you need, see the Animal Husbandry page.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Animals can be grouped into a few different categories: Predators, Prey, and Aquatic animals. The next few pages will detail each of these categories of animals.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Predators are animals that can attack the player. They are either Nocturnal, only hunting at night, or Diurnal, only hunting during the day. Predators can be neutral or hostile, depending on if they have killed a target recently. Predators have a home territory that they will defend - if you run far enough away, the predator will stop chasing and return home.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Polar Bear",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The polar bear spawns in only the coldest regions, temperature at most 10°C, with a rainfall of at least 100mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Grizzly Bear",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The grizzly bear spawns in forests of moderate climates, with a temperature range of -15 to 15°C and at least 200mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Black Bear",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The black bear spawns in forests of warmer, wetter climates, of temperature 5 to 20°C and at least 250mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The cougar prefers most moderate climates, with temperature from -10 to 21°C and at least 150mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The panther prefers most moderate climates, with temperature from -10 to 21°C and at least 150mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The lion spawns in plains with an average temperature of at least 16°C, and rainfall between 50 and 300mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The tiger spawns in forests with an average temperature of at least 13°C, and rainfall above 100mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The sabertooth spawns at any temperature above 0°C, and any rainfall above 250mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The wolf spawns at any temperature below 22°C, and rainfall between 70 and 480mm. They hunt in packs. Feeding a wolf enough times will tame it into a Dog.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The direwolf spawns at any temperature below freezing, and rainfall between 150 and 420mm. They hunt in packs. They are larger than regular wolves, and are not tamable.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The hyena spawns at any temperature warmer than 15°C, and rainfall between 80 and 380mm. They hunt in packs. They are not tamable.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The crocodile spawns near rivers, lakes, and marshes at any temperature warmer than 15°C, and at any rainfall. They are nocturnal, and most dangerous in the water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The ocelot attacks small animals. It spawns at any temperature from 15 to 30°C, and rainfall between 300 and 500mm, in forests. Feeding an ocelot enough times will tame it into a Cat. Ocelots can eat raw fish",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Ramming animals occasionally attempt to charge nearby creatures, including the player. Their attacks are powerful, but can be dodged. They will ram more frequently if attacked.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The boar spawns at temperature below 25°C and above -5°C, and rainfall between 130 and 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The moose spawns in forests at temperature below 10°C and above -15°C, and rainfall between 150 and 300mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The wildebeest spawns in open plains at any temperature above 13°C, and rainfall between 90 and 380mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The bison spawns in open plains at temperature below 15°C and above -10°C, and rainfall between 160 and 420mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Prey animals fear players and predators. They are adept at fleeing from danger, but generally cannot fight back. Some prey animals enjoy snacking on crops.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The rabbit is known to chew on carrots and cabbage. They are ubiquitous in the world, changing their coat based on climate. They only need 15mm of rainfall to spawn.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The fox likes to eat the berries off of bushes. It can be found in forests with temperature below 25°C, and rainfall between 130 and 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The deer spawns in forests at any temperature below 25°C and above -15°C, and rainfall between 130 and 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The caribou spawns at any temperature below -9°C, and rainfall between 110 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The gazelle spawns in open plains at any temperature above 12°C, and rainfall between 90 and 380mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.27.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The bongo spawns in forests at any temperature above 15°C, and rainfall between 230 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The grouse spawns at any temperature below 13°C and above -12°C, and rainfall between 150 and 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The pheasant spawns in forests at any temperature below 17°C and above -5°C, and rainfall between 100 and 300mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.30.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The turkey spawns in forests at any temperature below 17°C and above 0°C, and rainfall between 250 and 450mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.31.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The peafowl spawns in forests at any temperature above 14°C, and rainfall between 190 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.32.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The lemming spawns at any temperature below -3°C and above -12°C, and rainfall between 160 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.33.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The jerboa spawns at any temperature below 25°C and above -3°C, and rainfall below 160mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.34.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The mongoose spawns at any temperature above 12°C, and rainfall between 160 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.35.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Aquatic Animals are a broad category which covers a number of different behaviors. They may be Shore Dwellers, Fish, Shellfish, or Large Aquatic Creatures",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.36.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Aquatic Animals",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.36.title",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Shore Animals only spawn on sea shores and spend some of their day swimming, and some walking on the beach. They are curious creatures, and will follow the player around, but cannot be tamed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.37.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The penguin spawns in only the coldest beaches, with temperature of at most -14°C and rainfall of at least 75mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.38.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Sea Turtle",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.39.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The sea turtle likes warm water. It spawns in temperature of at least 21°C and rainfall of at least 250mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.39.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Fish are small creatures that swim in water. Most of them can be fished. Some prefer oceans, rivers, or lakes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.40.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Cod prefer colder oceans, temperature at most 18°C. They can be fished.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.41.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Pufferfish live in any ocean with at least a temperature of 10°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.42.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Jellyfish live in warmer oceans, with a temperature of at least 18°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.43.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Tropical Fish",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.44.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Tropical fish prefer warmer oceans, with a temperature of at least 18°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.44.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Salmon spawn in any river or lake with a temperature of at least -5°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.45.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Bluegill spawn in any river or lake with a temperature of at least -10°C and at most 26°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.46.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Shellfish are small animals that live on the floor of bodies of water. They cannot be fished, but drop shells that can be eaten or made into flux. Shellfish can be bait for fish.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.48.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Isopods spawn in deeper sections of oceans of temperature at most 14°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.49.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Lobster spawn in any ocean that is at most of a temperature of 21°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.50.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Crayfish are like lobster, but spawn in rivers and lakes. They need a temperature of at least 5°C and a rainfall of at least 125mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.51.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Horseshoe Crab",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.52.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Horseshoe crabs spawn in oceans of moderate climate, temperature between 10 and 21°C and with a rainfall of at most 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.52.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Large Water Creatures are larger animals that live in the bigger bodies of water. Some of them hunt fish. They drop blubber, which can be made into lamp fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.54.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Large Water Creatures",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.54.title",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Orca whales live in deep oceans with temperature of at most 19°C and rainfall of at least 100mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.55.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Dolphins live in deep oceans with temperature of at least 10°C and rainfall of at least 200mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.56.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Manatees live in any warm lake, with temperature of at least 20°C and rainfall of at most 300mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.57.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        }
      ],
